\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{initialize\_mats\_new()}{initialize\_mats\_new()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{initialize__matrices_8f90_a36d109657dabf879552204071465db10} 
subroutine initialize\+\_\+mats\+\_\+new}



Initializes main matrices for self-\/consistent DFT calculations. 

This subroutine initializes the overlap and Hamiltonian matrices used in self-\/consistent field calculations. It handles both real and complex wavefunctions, supports parallel execution with SCALAPACK, and includes double mesh capabilities for improved accuracy around atomic positions. The matrices are computed by integrating over the real-\/space grid. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{initialize__matrices_8f90_source_l00029}{29}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{initialize__matrices_8f90_source}{initialize\+\_\+matrices.\+f90}}.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{prepw()}{prepw()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{initialize__matrices_8f90_a59be3ab7597e81ced5edb12f91a05c94} 
subroutine prepw (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{n}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(n)}]{w}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Computes integration weights for finite difference schemes. 

This subroutine generates integration weights for numerical integration using finite difference schemes. The weights are designed for accurate integration of functions over grid points, with special handling for different numbers of grid points. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em n} & Number of grid points \\
\hline
{\em w} & Output\+: Integration weights array (n) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

subroutine set\+\_\+interval (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{scalea}{, }\item[{integer}]{nx0r}{, }\item[{integer}]{ny0r}{, }\item[{integer}]{nz0r}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3)}]{rion}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3)}]{rion\+\_\+upload}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{ax}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{ay}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{az}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxr}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyr}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzr}{, }\item[{logical}]{changer}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{weightx}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{weighty}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{weightz}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Sets grid intervals and weights for atomic-\/centered regions. 

This subroutine determines the grid intervals around atomic positions for computing matrix elements. It handles both position changes and mesh refinement, computes integration weights, and manages periodic boundary conditions. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em scalea} & Scaling factor for mesh refinement \\
\hline
{\em nx0r} & Number of grid points in x-\/direction around atom \\
\hline
{\em ny0r} & Number of grid points in y-\/direction around atom \\
\hline
{\em nz0r} & Number of grid points in z-\/direction around atom \\
\hline
{\em rion} & Atomic position \\
\hline
{\em rion\+\_\+upload} & Reference position for grid alignment \\
\hline
{\em ax} & Grid spacing in x-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em ay} & Grid spacing in y-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em az} & Grid spacing in z-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em nxi} & Output\+: Starting x-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nxf} & Output\+: Ending x-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nyi} & Output\+: Starting y-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nyf} & Output\+: Ending y-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nzi} & Output\+: Starting z-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nzf} & Output\+: Ending z-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nxr} & Output\+: Relative x-\/position in grid units \\
\hline
{\em nyr} & Output\+: Relative y-\/position in grid units \\
\hline
{\em nzr} & Output\+: Relative z-\/position in grid units \\
\hline
{\em changer} & Logical flag indicating position change \\
\hline
{\em weightx} & Output\+: Integration weights in x-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em weighty} & Output\+: Integration weights in y-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em weightz} & Output\+: Integration weights in z-\/direction \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{upload\_mat()}{upload\_mat()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{initialize__matrices_8f90_a5977e7c2f5f5e2aa58b21d17943a2b56} 
subroutine initialize\+\_\+mats\+\_\+new\+::upload\+\_\+mat (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{nxi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzf}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{ax}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{ay}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{az}{, }\item[{logical}]{add}{, }\item[{logical}]{memlarge}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Uploads matrix elements for a specific grid region. 

This subroutine processes a rectangular grid region and computes matrix elements for overlap and Hamiltonian matrices. It handles both real and complex wavefunctions, supports parallel execution, and includes contraction of basis functions when needed. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em nxi} & Starting x-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nxf} & Ending x-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nyi} & Starting y-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nyf} & Ending y-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nzi} & Starting z-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em nzf} & Ending z-\/index of the grid region \\
\hline
{\em ax} & Grid spacing in x-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em ay} & Grid spacing in y-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em az} & Grid spacing in z-\/direction \\
\hline
{\em add} & Logical flag to add to existing matrices \\
\hline
{\em memlarge} & Logical flag for large memory usage \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
